```python
"""
Search for a low-peak nonnegative step sequence.

Strategy:
- Represent candidates either as general nonnegative arrays or as
  anti-palindromic arrays a_i + a_{n-1-i} = 2, which are very effective here.
- Use a few cheap low-dimensional block searches to seed good profiles.
- Use a local linear-programming direction: given a, solve
      max sum(b)  subject to b >= 0, conv(a, b)_k <= M
  for M near the current peak.  Rescale b to the same total mass and blend.
- Polish with multi-basin hill climbing using FFT-evaluated objectives and
  occasionally subgradient moves for anti-palindromic arrays.
- Finish by resampling the best profiles to several lengths and verifying the
  top candidates with the authoritative evaluate_sequence() call.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    _HAVE_LINPROG = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_LINPROG = False

_fft_cache = {}


def _fft_size(m):
    key = int(m)
    if key in _fft_cache:
        return _fft_cache[key]
    size = 1
    while size < key:
        size <<= 1
    _fft_cache[key] = size
    return size


def _fft_convolve(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    size = _fft_size(m)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=size)[:m]
    return c


def _internal_objective(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    c = _fft_convolve(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def _normalize_positive(a, target_sum=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    if target_sum is None:
        target_sum = float(a.size)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-12:
        a = np.full(a.shape, target_sum / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(np.sum(a))
    a *= target_sum / s
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s > 0.0:
        a *= target_sum / s
    else:
        a = np.full(a.shape, target_sum / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a


def _sanitize_sequence(a):
    return _normalize_positive(a, float(np.asarray(a).size))


def _anti_from_left(left):
    left = np.asarray(left, dtype=np.float64)
    left = np.where(np.isfinite(left), left, 0.0)
    left = np.clip(left, 0.0, 2.0)
    h = left.size
    if h == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.empty(2 * h, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:h] = left
    a[h:] = 2.0 - left[::-1]
    return np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)


def _project_left_from_full(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    h = n // 2
    if h == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    rev = a[n - 1::-1][:h]
    left = (a[:h] - rev + 2.0) / 2.0
    left = np.where(np.isfinite(left), left, 0.0)
    left = np.clip(left, 0.0, 2.0)
    return left


def _block_from_vars(v, repeat):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 2.0)
    repeat = int(max(1, repeat))
    left = np.repeat(v, repeat)
    return _anti_from_left(left)


def _initial_block_vector(m, kind, rng):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    if np.issubdtype(type(kind), np.number):
        kind = int(kind)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    if kind == 0:
        v = np.full(m, 4.0 / 3.0, dtype=np.float64)
    elif kind == 1:
        v = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
        half = m // 2
        v[:half] = 1.3
        v[half:] = 0.7
    elif kind == 2:
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 0.9))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        v = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x + phase)
    elif kind == 3:
        v = rng.uniform(0.2, 1.8, m).astype(np.float64)
    elif kind == 4:
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.75))
        signs = np.where(np.arange(m) % 2 == 0, 1.0, -1.0)
        v = 1.0 + amp * signs
    elif kind == 5:
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 0.9))
        v = 1.0 + amp * (2.0 * x - 1.0)
    else:
        v = np.full(m, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 1.0)
    return np.clip(v, 0.0, 2.0)


def _perturb_block_vector(v, rng, fraction, sigma):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if v.size == 0:
        return v
    mask = rng.random(v.size) < fraction
    if mask.any():
        idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
        if float(rng.random()) < 0.5:
            v[idx] = np.clip(v[idx] + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, idx.size), 0.0, 2.0)
        else:
            v[idx] = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0, idx.size)
    return v


def _block_candidate(v, rng):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    m = v.size
    cand = v.copy()
    if m == 0:
        return cand
    r = float(rng.random())
    if r < 0.40:
        idx = int(rng.integers(m))
        cand[idx] = np.clip(cand[idx] + float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.2)), 0.0, 2.0)
    elif r < 0.80:
        maxw = min(m, max(2, m // 3))
        w = int(rng.integers(1, maxw + 1))
        idx = np.sort(rng.choice(m, w, replace=False))
        cand[idx] = np.clip(cand[idx] + rng.normal(0.0, 0.2, idx.size), 0.0, 2.0)
    else:
        w = int(rng.integers(1, min(3, m) + 1))
        idx = rng.choice(m, w, replace=False)
        for i in idx:
            if float(rng.random()) < 0.7:
                cand[i] = np.clip(cand[i] + float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.3)), 0.0, 2.0)
            else:
                cand[i] = float(rng.choice(np.array([0.0, 1.0, 2.0], dtype=np.float64)))
    return np.clip(cand, 0.0, 2.0)


def _block_basins(initial_vectors, repeat, deadline, rng, max_basins=5, threshold=400):
    vecs = []
    seqs = []
    scores = []
    for v in initial_vectors:
        v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
        v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 1.0)
        v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 2.0)
        a = _block_from_vars(v, repeat)
        s = _internal_objective(a)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            vecs.append(v)
            seqs.append(a)
            scores.append(float(s))
    if len(vecs) == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64), np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64), np.inf
    order = np.argsort(np.array(scores, dtype=np.float64))
    selected = []
    for idx in order:
        if len(selected) >= max_basins:
            break
        selected.append(int(idx))
    basins = [vecs[i].copy() for i in selected]
    basin_scores = [scores[i] for i in selected]
    best_idx = selected[0]
    best_v = vecs[best_idx].copy()
    best_a = seqs[best_idx].copy()
    best_s = float(scores[best_idx])
    stagnation = [0] * len(basins)
    b_size = best_v.size
    next_print = time.monotonic() + 10.0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if len(basins) == 0:
            break
        if float(rng.random()) < 0.5:
            idx = int(np.argmin(np.array(basin_scores, dtype=np.float64)))
        else:
            idx = int(rng.integers(len(basins)))
        cand = _block_candidate(basins[idx], rng)
        a_cand = _block_from_vars(cand, repeat)
        s_cand = _internal_objective(a_cand)
        if np.isfinite(s_cand) and s_cand < basin_scores[idx]:
            basins[idx] = cand
            basin_scores[idx] = s_cand
            stagnation[idx] = 0
            if s_cand < best_s:
                best_v = cand.copy()
                best_a = a_cand.copy()
                best_s = s_cand
        else:
            stagnation[idx] += 1
            if stagnation[idx] > threshold:
                choice = float(rng.random())
                if choice < 0.50:
                    new_v = _perturb_block_vector(best_v, rng, float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.3)), 0.3)
                elif choice < 0.75:
                    new_v = _perturb_block_vector(basins[idx], rng, 0.5, 0.5)
                else:
                    new_v = _initial_block_vector(b_size, int(rng.integers(0, 6)), rng)
                new_a = _block_from_vars(new_v, repeat)
                new_s = _internal_objective(new_a)
                basins[idx] = new_v
                basin_scores[idx] = new_s
                stagnation[idx] = 0
                if np.isfinite(new_s) and new_s < best_s:
                    best_v = new_v.copy()
                    best_a = new_a.copy()
                    best_s = new_s
        if time.monotonic() > next_print:
            print("block basin best %.6f" % best_s)
            next_print = time.monotonic() + 15.0
    return best_v, best_a, best_s


def _run_block_phase(deadline, rng):
    results = []
    bs = [2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64]
    total_bs = len(bs)
    for bi, b in enumerate(bs):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 5.0:
            break
        per = max(5.0, rem / max(1, total_bs - bi))
        local_deadline = time.monotonic() + per
        repeat = max(1, 256 // b)
        inits = []
        for kind in range(6):
            inits.append(_initial_block_vector(b, kind, rng))
        v, a, s = _block_basins(inits, repeat, local_deadline, rng, max_basins=4, threshold=300)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            results.append((b, v, a, float(s)))
            print("block B=%d local best %.6f" % (b, s))
    return results


def _lp_direction(a, M):
    if not _HAVE_LINPROG:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n < 16 or n > 1200:
        return None
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None
    C = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        C[j:j + n, j] = a
    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, float(M), dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=C,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True},
        )
        if res.success and res.x is not None:
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
            x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(np.sum(x)) > 1e-12:
                return x
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=C, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds)
        if res.success and res.x is not None:
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
            x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(np.sum(x)) > 1e-12:
                return x
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def _lp_optimize_sequence(a, deadline, rng):
    a = _normalize_positive(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), float(a.size))
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = _internal_objective(a)
    if not _HAVE_LINPROG or n < 32:
        return best_a, best_s
    a = best_a.copy()
    no_improve = 0
    m_factor = 1.0
    fail_count = 0
    next_print = time.monotonic() + 20.0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
        c = _fft_convolve(a)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break
        b = _lp_direction(a, M * m_factor)
        if b is None:
            fail_count += 1
            if m_factor > 0.7:
                m_factor -= 0.05
            elif fail_count > 3:
                break
            continue
        fail_count = 0
        sum_b = float(np.sum(b))
        if sum_b <= 1e-10:
            m_factor = min(1.0, m_factor + 0.05)
            continue
        g = b * (float(np.sum(a)) / sum_b)
        g = _normalize_positive(g, n)
        best_cand = a
        best_cand_s = best_s
        for t in (0.5, 0.35, 0.25, 0.15, 0.08, 0.04, 0.02):
            cand = (1.0 - t) * a + t * g
            cand = _normalize_positive(cand, n)
            s = _internal_objective(cand)
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_cand_s:
                best_cand = cand
                best_cand_s = s
        if best_cand_s < best_s - 1e-10:
            a = best_cand.copy()
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_s = best_cand_s
            no_improve = 0
            m_factor = min(1.0, m_factor + 0.02)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve == 2:
                m_factor = max(0.85, m_factor - 0.05)
            elif no_improve == 5:
                m_factor = 0.80
            elif no_improve >= 14:
                break
        if time.monotonic() > next_print:
            print("LP n=%d score=%.6f factor=%.2f" % (n, best_s, m_factor))
            next_print = time.monotonic() + 30.0
    return best_a, best_s


def _resample_array(a, n_target):
    a = _normalize_positive(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), float(a.size) if a.size else 1.0)
    n_target = int(max(1, n_target))
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n_target, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == n_target:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_target, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return _normalize_positive(out, float(n_target))


def _resample_left(left, h_target):
    left = np.asarray(left, dtype=np.float64)
    left = np.where(np.isfinite(left), left, 1.0)
    left = np.clip(left, 0.0, 2.0)
    h_target = int(max(0, h_target))
    if h_target == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if left.size == 0:
        return np.full(h_target, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    if left.size == h_target:
        return left.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, left.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, h_target, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, left)
    return np.clip(out, 0.0, 2.0)


def _full_left_from_block(v, h_target):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 1.0)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 2.0)
    h_target = int(max(0, h_target))
    if h_target == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size == 0:
        return np.full(h_target, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    if h_target % v.size == 0:
        return np.repeat(v, h_target // v.size)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, v.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, h_target, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.clip(np.interp(x_new, x_old, v), 0.0, 2.0)


def _random_general_array(n, rng):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    a = rng.normal(1.0, 0.3, n)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    if float(np.sum(a)) <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return _normalize_positive(a, float(n))


def _perturb_full(a, rng, fraction, sigma):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    mask = rng.random(n) < fraction
    if mask.any():
        idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
        if float(rng.random()) < 0.5:
            a[idx] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, idx.size))
        else:
            a[idx] += rng.normal(0.0, 0.01 + 0.1 * sigma, idx.size)
    return _normalize_positive(a, float(n))


def _log_width(rng, minw, maxw):
    minw = int(max(1, minw))
    maxw = int(max(minw, maxw))
    if maxw <= 1:
        return 1
    val = float(np.exp(rng.uniform(float(np.log(minw)), float(np.log(maxw)))))
    return int(min(maxw, max(1, 1 + int(np.floor(val)))))


def _subgrad_candidate(left, n, K, rng):
    left = np.asarray(left, dtype=np.float64)
    left = np.where(np.isfinite(left), left, 1.0)
    left = np.clip(left, 0.0, 2.0)
    n = int(n)
    h = n // 2
    if h <= 0:
        return left
    a = _anti_from_left(left)
    c = _fft_convolve(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return left
    K = int(min(max(1, K), c.size - 1))
    top = np.argsort(c)[-K:]
    g = np.zeros(h, dtype=np.float64)
    for kk in top:
        k = int(kk)
        lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        hi = min(h - 1, k)
        if hi >= lo:
            jmax = k - lo
            jmin = k - hi
            g[lo:hi + 1] += a[jmax:jmin - 1:-1]
        lo2 = max(0, n - 1 - k)
        hi2 = min(h - 1, 2 * n - 2 - k)
        if hi2 >= lo2:
            jmin2 = k - n + 1 + lo2
            jmax2 = k - n + 1 + hi2
            g[lo2:hi2 + 1] -= a[jmin2:jmax2 + 1]
    g *= 2.0
    gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
    if gmax <= 1e-12:
        if rng is not None:
            left = np.clip(left + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, h), 0.0, 2.0)
        return left
    scale_options = np.array([0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20], dtype=np.float64)
    scale = float(rng.choice(scale_options)) if rng is not None else 0.05
    lr = scale / gmax
    new_left = left - lr * g
    new_left = np.clip(new_left, 0.0, 2.0)
    if np.all(new_left == left):
        if rng is not None:
            new_left = np.clip(left + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, h), 0.0, 2.0)
    return new_left


def _full_candidate(a, sigma, rng):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    r = float(rng.random())
    sigma = float(sigma)
    if r < 0.30:
        left = _project_left_from_full(a)
        left = _subgrad_candidate(left, n, int(rng.integers(4, 17)), rng)
        return _normalize_positive(_anti_from_left(left), float(n))
    cand = a.copy()
    if r < 0.60:
        maxw = max(4, n // 64)
        w = min(n, _log_width(rng, 1, maxw))
        s = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
        if float(rng.random()) < 0.5:
            cand[s:s + w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, w))
        else:
            cand[s:s + w] += rng.normal(0.0, 0.05 + 0.1 * sigma, w)
    elif r < 0.80:
        maxw = max(2, n // 256)
        w = min(n, int(rng.integers(1, maxw + 1)))
        s = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
        base = np.clip(cand[s:s + w], 0.0, None)
        if float(rng.random()) < 0.60:
            cand[s:s + w] = np.clip(base + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, w), 0.0, None)
        else:
            mean = float(base.mean()) if base.size > 0 else 1.0
            cand[s:s + w] = np.clip(rng.normal(mean, 0.2, w), 0.0, None)
    elif r < 0.90:
        maxw = max(3, n // 128)
        w = min(n, int(rng.integers(1, maxw + 1)))
        s = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
        mean = float(cand[s:s + w].mean()) if w > 0 else 1.0
        cand[s:s + w] = rng.uniform(0.0, max(0.2, mean), w)
    else:
        if float(rng.random()) < 0.5:
            cand = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
        else:
            left = _project_left_from_full(a)
            left = np.clip(left + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, left.size), 0.0, 2.0)
            cand = _anti_from_left(left)
    return _normalize_positive(cand, float(n))


def _full_basins(initial_sequences, deadline, rng, max_basins=8, threshold=800):
    if len(initial_sequences) == 0:
        return None, np.inf
    base_n = int(np.asarray(initial_sequences[0]).size)
    if base_n <= 0:
        return None, np.inf
    processed = []
    scores = []
    for seq in initial_sequences:
        arr = _resample_array(seq, base_n)
        arr = _normalize_positive(arr, float(base_n))
        sc = _internal_objective(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            processed.append(arr)
            scores.append(float(sc))
    if len(processed) == 0:
        return None, np.inf
    order = np.argsort(np.array(scores, dtype=np.float64))
    selected = []
    for idx in order:
        if len(selected) >= max_basins:
            break
        selected.append(int(idx))
    basins = [processed[i].copy() for i in selected]
    basin_scores = [scores[i] for i in selected]
    best_idx = selected[0]
    best_a = processed[best_idx].copy()
    best_s = float(scores[best_idx])
    stagnation = [0] * len(basins)
    sigma = 0.10
    next_print = time.monotonic() + 20.0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if len(basins) == 0:
            break
        if float(rng.random()) < 0.60:
            idx = int(np.argmin(np.array(basin_scores, dtype=np.float64)))
        else:
            idx = int(rng.integers(len(basins)))
        cand = _full_candidate(basins[idx], sigma, rng)
        sc = _internal_objective(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < basin_scores[idx]:
            basins[idx] = cand
            basin_scores[idx] = sc
            stagnation[idx] = 0
            if sc < best_s:
                best_a = cand.copy()
                best_s = sc
        else:
            stagnation[idx] += 1
            if stagnation[idx] > threshold:
                choice = float(rng.random())
                frac = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.25))
                if choice < 0.45:
                    new_a = _perturb_full(best_a, rng, frac, 0.30)
                elif choice < 0.70:
                    new_a = _perturb_full(basins[idx], rng, 0.5, 0.40)
                else:
                    new_a = _random_general_array(base_n, rng)
                new_sc = _internal_objective(new_a)
                basins[idx] = new_a
                basin_scores[idx] = new_sc
                stagnation[idx] = 0
                if np.isfinite(new_sc) and new_sc < best_s:
                    best_a = new_a.copy()
                    best_s = new_sc
        if time.monotonic() > next_print:
            print("full basin best %.6f" % best_s)
            next_print = time.monotonic() + 25.0
    return best_a, best_s


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    total = int(max(20.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0))
    deadline = start + float(total)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    pv = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    try:
        p = np.asarray(pv, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        if p.size >= 32 and float(np.sum(p)) > 0.01:
            parent = _normalize_positive(p, float(p.size))
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    block_deadline = start + min(90.0, max(10.0, float(total) * 0.06))
    block_deadline = max(start + 5.0, min(block_deadline, deadline - 120.0))
    block_results = _run_block_phase(block_deadline, rng)

    best_lp_a = None
    best_lp_s = np.inf

    if _HAVE_LINPROG and time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0:
        n_lp = 512
        lp_starts = []
        if len(block_results) > 0:
            br_scores = []
            for item in block_results:
                br_scores.append(item[3])
            order = np.argsort(np.array(br_scores, dtype=np.float64))
            count = 0
            for idx in order:
                b, v, a, s = block_results[int(idx)]
                left = _full_left_from_block(v, n_lp // 2)
                arr = _anti_from_left(left)
                arr = _normalize_positive(arr, float(n_lp))
                lp_starts.append(arr)
                count += 1
                if count >= 3:
                    break
        h_lp = n_lp // 2
        lp_starts.append(_anti_from_left(np.full(h_lp, 4.0 / 3.0, dtype=np.float64)))
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, h_lp, dtype=np.float64)
        lp_starts.append(_anti_from_left(np.clip(1.0 + 0.6 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x), 0.0, 2.0)))
        lp_starts.append(_anti_from_left(np.clip(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.8, h_lp), 0.0, 2.0)))
        if parent is not None and parent.size >= 100:
            lp_starts.append(_resample_array(parent, n_lp))
        lp_starts = lp_starts[:4]

        lp_deadline = min(deadline - 300.0, time.monotonic() + min(420.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.40))
        remaining = len(lp_starts)
        for arr in lp_starts:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 60.0:
                break
            local_deadline = min(lp_deadline, time.monotonic() + max(30.0, rem / max(1, remaining)))
            if local_deadline > time.monotonic() + 30.0:
                a_new, s_new = _lp_optimize_sequence(arr, local_deadline, rng)
                if np.isfinite(s_new) and s_new < best_lp_s:
                    best_lp_a = a_new
                    best_lp_s = s_new
            remaining -= 1

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if best_lp_a is not None and rem > 700.0 and _HAVE_LINPROG:
            lp2_deadline = min(deadline - 360.0, time.monotonic() + min(300.0, rem * 0.25))
            if lp2_deadline > time.monotonic() + 60.0:
                a2 = _resample_array(best_lp_a, 1024)
                a2s, s2 = _lp_optimize_sequence(a2, lp2_deadline, rng)
                if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_lp_s:
                    best_lp_a = a2s
                    best_lp_s = s2

    if best_lp_a is None:
        if len(block_results) > 0:
            br_scores = []
            for item in block_results:
                br_scores.append(item[3])
            idx = int(np.argmin(np.array(br_scores, dtype=np.float64)))
            b, v, a, s = block_results[idx]
            best_lp_a = _resample_array(a, 1024)
            best_lp_s = _internal_objective(best_lp_a)
        else:
            best_lp_a = _random_general_array(512, rng)
            best_lp_s = _internal_objective(best_lp_a)

    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 800.0:
        full_n = 4096
    else:
        full_n = 2048
    h_full = full_n // 2
    full_starts = []
    full_starts.append(_resample_array(best_lp_a, full_n))
    left_proj = _project_left_from_full(best_lp_a)
    full_starts.append(_normalize_positive(_anti_from_left(_resample_left(left_proj, h_full)), float(full_n)))
    if len(block_results) > 0:
        br_scores = []
        for item in block_results:
            br_scores.append(item[3])
        order = np.argsort(np.array(br_scores, dtype=np.float64))
        count = 0
        for idx in order:
            b, v, a, s = block_results[int(idx)]
            left = _full_left_from_block(v, h_full)
            full_starts.append(_normalize_positive(_anti_from_left(left), float(full_n)))
            count += 1
            if count >= 3:
                break
    full_starts.append(_anti_from_left(np.full(h_full, 4.0 / 3.0, dtype=np.float64)))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, h_full, dtype=np.float64)
    full_starts.append(_anti_from_left(np.clip(1.0 + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x), 0.0, 2.0)))
    if parent is not None and parent.size >= 100:
        full_starts.append(_resample_array(parent, full_n))

    full_deadline = max(time.monotonic() + 10.0, deadline - 60.0)
    best_full, _ = _full_basins(full_starts, full_deadline, rng, max_basins=8, threshold=800)
    if best_full is None:
        best_full = _resample_array(best_lp_a, full_n)

    best_anti_left = _resample_left(_project_left_from_full(best_full), h_full)

    final_candidates = []
    targets = set()
    targets.add(full_n)
    if 2 * full_n <= 10000:
        targets.add(2 * full_n)
    targets.add(8192)
    if parent is not None:
        pl = int(parent.size)
        if pl % 2 == 1:
            pl -= 1
        if 800 <= pl <= 10000:
            targets.add(pl)
    for t in sorted(targets):
        if t < 64:
            continue
        ag = _resample_array(best_full, int(t))
        sg = _internal_objective(ag)
        if np.isfinite(sg):
            final_candidates.append((ag, float(sg)))
        h_t = int(t) // 2
        if h_t > 0:
            aa = _anti_from_left(_resample_left(best_anti_left, h_t))
            sa = _internal_objective(aa)
            if np.isfinite(sa):
                final_candidates.append((aa, float(sa)))

    if len(final_candidates) == 0:
        final_seq = _sanitize_sequence(best_full)
    else:
        scores = []
        for item in final_candidates:
            scores.append(item[1])
        order = np.argsort(np.array(scores, dtype=np.float64))
        final_seq = final_candidates[int(order[0])][0]

    best_true_score = np.inf
    best_true_seq = final_seq
    if len(final_candidates) > 0:
        scores = []
        for item in final_candidates:
            scores.append(item[1])
        order = np.argsort(np.array(scores, dtype=np.float64))
        checked = 0
        big_checked = 0
        for idx in order:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
                break
            if checked >= 4:
                break
            cand = final_candidates[int(idx)][0]
            if cand.size > 8000:
                if big_checked >= 1:
                    break
                big_checked += 1
            try:
                ts = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in cand]))
                if np.isfinite(ts) and ts < best_true_score:
                    best_true_score = ts
                    best_true_seq = cand
            except Exception:
                pass
            checked += 1

    final_seq = best_true_seq if best_true_score < np.inf else final_seq
    final_seq = _sanitize_sequence(final_seq)
    if final_seq.size > 12000:
        final_seq = _resample_array(final_seq, 8192)
    return [float(x) for x in final_seq]
```
